# Solved PYQs — Subject Index

**2019–2026 released corpus:** 3,416 solved answerable units · 3,600 stable routed anchors · 353 canonical topic pages.

Counts are generated from `catalogue/CORPUS-METADATA.json`. Topic pages are catalogue-only and link to canonical paper-wise solutions. Marks are represented marks across every solved selectable alternative; they are not attempted-paper totals.
